 for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(art))document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--v020-'+k,'url("'+v+'")');
 const reduce=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
 // Interactive depth: slow focal shift, no device sensors, respects reduced motion.
 const hero=document.querySelector('.hero.chronicle-hero');
 const map=document.querySelector('.story-hero');
 function camera(el){if(!el)return;
  const motion=e=>{if(reduce.matches)return;const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();const x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width-.5,y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height-.5;
   el.style.setProperty('--cam-x',Math.round(-x*18)+'px');el.style.setProperty('--cam-y',Math.round(-y*18)+'px');};
  el.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(e.pointerType==='mouse')motion(e)},{passive:true});
  el.addEventListener('pointerleave',()=>{el.style.setProperty('--cam-x','0px');el.style.setProperty('--cam-y','0px')});
 }
 camera(hero);camera(map);
 const app=document.querySelector('#app');
 if(!app)return;
 let scheduled=false;
 function refresh(){
  const scene=app.querySelector('.stage-viewport .stage-scene .visual-frame');if(!scene)return;
  let canvas=scene.querySelector('canvas');if(!canvas)return;
  if(!scene.querySelector('.v020-world-label')){
   const label=document.createElement('span');label.className='v020-world-label';label.textContent='✧ 装置を観察';scene.append(label);
  }
  const viewport=scene.closest('.stage-viewport');
  if(viewport&&!viewport.querySelector('.v020-ambience')){const amb=document.createElement('div');amb.className='v020-ambience';viewport.prepend(amb)}
  let d=scene.querySelector('.v020-direct-panel');
  if(!d){d=document.createElement('div');d.className='v020-direct-panel';d.innerHTML='<span class="v020-drag-label">↕ 指で振り子を上下に</span><button type="button" data-v020="short" aria-label="糸を5cm短くする">−</button><span>糸の長さ</span><output aria-live="off"></output><button type="button" data-v020="long" aria-label="糸を5cm長くする">＋</button>';scene.append(d);
   d.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const slider=app.querySelector('#rng-length');if(!slider)return;
    const dir=b.dataset.v020==='short'?-1:1;updateLength(slider,Number(slider.value)+dir*5);
   }));
  }
  const slider=app.querySelector('#rng-length');
  d.hidden=!slider;
  if(slider)d.querySelector('output').textContent=slider.value+'cm';
  // Canvas is recreated between stages; reinstall direct manipulation without touching physics.
  if(!canvas.dataset.v020Direct){canvas.dataset.v020Direct='1';
   let drag=null;
   canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{const sl=app.querySelector('#rng-length');if(!sl||e.button>0)return;
    drag={y:e.clientY,start:Number(sl.value)};try{canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch{};
   });
   canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!drag)return;const sl=app.querySelector('#rng-length');if(!sl)return;
    const change=Math.round((e.clientY-drag.y)/2.1);
    updateLength(sl,drag.start+change);
   });
   for(const ev of ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'])canvas.addEventListener(ev,()=>{drag=null});
  }
  if(viewport&&!viewport.querySelector('.v020-pan-controls')){
   const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='v020-pan-controls';
   bar.innerHTML='<span class="v020-pan-tip">視点</span><button type="button" data-pan="-1" aria-label="左側を見渡す">◀</button><button type="button" data-pan="0" aria-label="正面を見る">◎</button><button type="button" data-pan="1" aria-label="右側を見渡す">▶</button>';
   bar.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-pan]');if(!b)return;const n=Number(b.dataset.pan);
    viewport.style.setProperty('--stage-cam-x',(n*18)+'px');viewport.style.setProperty('--stage-cam-y',n===0?'0px':'5px');
   });viewport.append(bar);
  }
 }
 function updateLength(el,val){const lo=Number(el.min),hi=Number(el.max);const step=Number(el.step)||1;
  const next=Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,lo+Math.round((val-lo)/step)*step));
  if(Number(el.value)!==next){el.value=String(next);el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}
  const out=app.querySelector('.v020-direct-panel output');if(out)out.textContent=el.value+'cm';
 }
 // observe top-level app render transitions: avoid observing own HUD edits.
 new MutationObserver(()=>{if(scheduled)return;scheduled=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scheduled=false;refresh()})}).observe(app,{childList:true,subtree:false});
 document.querySelectorAll('.tabs .tab').forEach(x=>x.addEventListener('click',()=>requestAnimationFrame(refresh)));
 refresh();
})();
</script>

<script id="immersive-play-v021">
(()=>{
 'use strict';
 const app=document.getElementById('app');if(!app)return;
 const $=(sel)=>app.querySelector(sel);
 let held=null, frameTick=0, currentCanvas=null;
 function mode(){return typeof stage==='function' && state.view==='world' ? stage().mode : ''}
 function slider(){
  if(mode()==='beatbridge')return $('#rng-bridgeLength');
  if(mode()==='pendulum')return $('#rng-length');
  return null;
 }
 function adjust(el,val){if(!el)return false;const lo=Number(el.min),hi=Number(el.max),step=Number(el.step)||1;
  const snapped=Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,lo+Math.round((val-lo)/step)*step));
  if(Number(el.value)===snapped)return false;
  el.value=String(snapped);el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));return true;}
 function ensureOverlay(canvas){
  if(!canvas||!canvas.parentElement)return;
  const frame=canvas.parentElement;
  if(!frame.querySelector('.v021-bob-target')){
   const target=document.createElement('button');target.type='button';target.className='v021-bob-target shine';
   target.setAttribute('aria-label','振り子のおもり。上下にドラッグして糸の長さを調整');target.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');target.tabIndex=-1;
   frame.append(target);
   target.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const sl=slider();if(!sl||e.button>0)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    held={id:e.pointerId,startY:e.clientY,start:Number(sl.value),el:target,slider:sl};
    target.classList.add('dragging');
    try{target.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}
   });
   target.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(!held||held.id!==e.pointerId)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const scale=mode()==='beatbridge'?70/Math.max(185,r.height):80/Math.max(185,r.height);
    adjust(held.slider,held.start+(e.clientY-held.startY)*scale);
   });
   function end(e){if(!held)return;held.el.classList.remove('dragging');held=null;}
   ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'].forEach(ev=>target.addEventListener(ev,end));
   target.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(!slider())return;if(e.key==='ArrowUp'||e.key==='ArrowDown'){
     e.preventDefault();adjust(slider(),Number(slider().value)+(e.key==='ArrowDown'?1:-1)*5);}});
   // The v0.20 implementation captured the entire canvas; stop that accidental adjustment.
   canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(slider())e.stopImmediatePropagation()},true);
  }
  if(!frame.querySelector('.v021-world-hud')){
   const hud=document.createElement('div');hud.className='v021-world-hud';hud.setAttribute('aria-label','装置操作ガイド');
   hud.innerHTML='<div class="v021-world-title"><span data-v021-name>振り子の調整</span><output data-v021-period>–</output></div><div class="v021-world-progress"><i></i></div><div class="v021-world-actions"><button type="button" data-v021-main>装置を起動</button><button type="button" class="secondary" data-v021-panel>詳細</button></div><div class="v021-world-hint" data-v021-tip>金色のおもりを上下に動かそう</div>';
   frame.append(hud);
   hud.querySelector('[data-v021-main]').addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const m=mode();const id=m==='beatbridge'?(state.bridgeReady?'#bridgePush':'#bridgeCalibrate'):m==='rhythm'?'#pushSwing':'#runStage';
      const button=$(id);if(button&&!button.disabled)button.click();
   });
   hud.querySelector('[data-v021-panel]').addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const btn=document.getElementById('immersiveControlsToggle')||document.querySelector('[data-immersive="controls"]')||document.querySelector('#openControls');
      if(btn)btn.click();else{
        const cmds=app.querySelector('.controls'); if(cmds){cmds.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'});}
      }
   });
  }
  if(!frame.querySelector('.v021-direct-note')){
   const tip=document.createElement('span');tip.className='v021-direct-note';tip.textContent='↕ おもりをドラッグ';frame.append(tip);
  }
 }
 function update(){
  const canvas=$('#gameCanvas');if(!canvas || state.view!=='world'){currentCanvas=null;return;}
  if(canvas!==currentCanvas){held=null;currentCanvas=canvas;ensureOverlay(canvas)}
  const frame=canvas.parentElement;if(!frame)return;
  const target=frame.querySelector('.v021-bob-target'),hud=frame.querySelector('.v021-world-hud'),note=frame.querySelector('.v021-direct-note');
  if(!target||!hud)return;
  const sl=slider(),m=mode();
  const visual=m==='beatbridge'||m==='pendulum'||m==='rhythm';
  hud.hidden=!visual;
  const canDrag=!!sl;
  target.setAttribute('aria-hidden',canDrag?'false':'true');target.tabIndex=canDrag?0:-1;
  note.hidden=!canDrag;
  if(!visual)return;
  const title=hud.querySelector('[data-v021-name]'),out=hud.querySelector('[data-v021-period]'),tip=hud.querySelector('[data-v021-tip]'),bar=hud.querySelector('.v021-world-progress i'),action=hud.querySelector('[data-v021-main]');
  const t=performance.now()/1000;
  if(m==='beatbridge'){
   const length=state.bridgeLength,T=period(length,10);
   title.textContent=state.bridgeReady?'歯車と橋を動かそう':'糸を調整 → 周期を測る';out.textContent=Number(T).toFixed(2)+'秒 / 2.00秒';
   bar.style.width=(state.bridgeReady?100:Math.max(0,100-Math.abs(T-2)*110))+'%';
   action.textContent=state.bridgeReady?'⚡ 中央でスイッチ':'▶ 周期を測る';
   const center=state.bridgeReady&&Math.abs(Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(t-state.bridgeStart)/T))>.75;
   action.disabled=state.bridgeHits>=3;
   tip.textContent=state.bridgeHits>=3?'橋が完成！ 歯車の動きを観察しよう':state.bridgeReady?(center?'今がチャンス！ 振り子が中央を通る':'おもりが中央へ来るのを待とう'):'金色のおもりをドラッグして糸の長さを変えよう';
  }else if(m==='pendulum'){
   const s=stage(),T=period(state.pValues.length,state.pValues.amp);
   title.textContent='周期を合わせよう';out.textContent=T.toFixed(2)+'秒 / '+Number(s.goal).toFixed(2)+'秒';
   bar.style.width=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,100-Math.abs(T-s.goal)*200))+'%';
   action.textContent='⚡ 仕掛けを動かす';action.disabled=false;
   tip.textContent='金色のおもりを上下にドラッグ。動いた長さと周期を比べよう';
  }else{
   title.textContent='共振のリズムを見極めよう';out.textContent=state.rhythmHit+' / 4 回';
   bar.style.width=state.rhythmHit*25+'%';action.textContent='✋ 中央の瞬間に押す';action.disabled=state.rhythmHit>=4;
   tip.textContent='中央を通る瞬間に押そう。揺れ幅が少しずつ大きくなる';
  }
  if(canDrag){
    const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(),fr=frame.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(r.width>0&&r.height>0){
      let x,y;
      if(m==='beatbridge'){
       const T=period(state.bridgeLength,10),dt=state.bridgeReady?t-state.bridgeStart:t;
       const a=.35*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*dt/T),L=145+(state.bridgeLength-60)*.85;
       x=175+L*.26;y=114+L;x=x/560*r.width;y=y/470*r.height;
      }else{
       const values=state.pValues,T=period(values.length,values.amp);
       const L=72+values.length*.89,a=values.amp*Math.PI/180*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*t/T);
       x=(canvas.width*.45+L*.22)/canvas.width*r.width;
       y=(36+L)/canvas.height*r.height;
      }
      target.style.left=(r.left-fr.left+x)+'px';target.style.top=(r.top-fr.top+y)+'px';
    }
  }
 }
 // For interaction, use the game's existing controls as single source of truth.
 const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{requestAnimationFrame(update)});
 observer.observe(app,{childList:true,subtree:false});
 const animateMarker=()=>{update();frameTick=requestAnimationFrame(animateMarker)};
 frameTick=requestAnimationFrame(animateMarker);
})();
</script>

 .exp22-foot-controls button{width:36px;height:35px}.exp22-interact{bottom:calc(167px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));font-size:11px;min-height:49px;padding:8px 11px}
 .exp22-note{bottom:calc(253px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));font-size:9px;max-width:126px}}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.exp22-character.walking .exp22-figure,.exp22-apparatus .exp22-sigil{animation:none!important}}
</style>
<script id="exploration-v022-script">
(() => {
 'use strict';
 // The original #gameCanvas, range sliders, success evaluation, stage state and saves are untouched.
 const app=document.getElementById('app');if(!app)return;
 const player={x:.49,z:.12,face:1};
 const machine={x:.76,z:.43};
 const held=new Set();let current=null,target=null,last=0,nearBefore=null,activeBefore=false;
  let stationOpen=false,pendingInteract=false,cameraX=0,cameraY=0,lastDirection='down';
 let exp28Destination=null,exp28LastBroadcast=0;
 const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.min(b,Math.max(a,v));
 const isActive=()=>document.body.dataset.scene==='world'&&typeof state!=='undefined'&&state.sid==='s01';
 function makeAvatar(){return `<div class="exp22-shadow"></div><div class="exp22-figure"><svg viewBox="0 0 112 165" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
 <defs>
  <linearGradient id="exp23-cloth" x1="0" y1="0" x2=".9" y2="1"><stop stop-color="#b5d8d1"/><stop offset=".38" stop-color="#51747c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#162b43"/></linearGradient>
  <linearGradient id="exp23-cape" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop stop-color="#a2bdc6"/><stop offset=".52" stop-color="#345165"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#101d32"/></linearGradient>
  <linearGradient id="exp23-metal" x1="0" y1="0" x2=".8" y2="1"><stop stop-color="#fff2c1"/><stop offset=".38" stop-color="#dcb66a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#815c3d"/></linearGradient>
  <radialGradient id="exp23-lantern"><stop stop-color="#fffde3"/><stop offset=".24" stop-color="#ffdf8e"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#f0ac4c" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
 </defs>
 <g class="exp23-cloak"><path d="M37 75 Q20 80 12 135 L36 138 Q45 122 51 98Z" fill="url(#exp23-cape)" stroke="#bbd2d4" stroke-opacity=".65" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M71 75 Q93 84 102 135 L73 138 L62 96Z" fill="#142a42" stroke="#b4bbbe" stroke-opacity=".5" stroke-width="2"/></g>
 <g class="exp23-leg exp23-leg-back"><path d="M52 108 L51 139 L65 140 L70 114Z" fill="#1a2c40" stroke="#97a3a0" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M48 135 L67 136 L74 148 Q63 151 46 144Z" fill="#56453c" stroke="#e0b96b" stroke-width="2"/></g>
 <g class="exp23-leg exp23-leg-front"><path d="M35 111 L37 138 L50 141 L56 115Z" fill="#1b3448" stroke="#96b5b7" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M34 134 L51 137 L55 148 Q42 151 30 144Z" fill="#5b493c" stroke="#e2c07e" stroke-width="2"/></g>
 <g class="exp23-arm exp23-arm-back"><path d="M32 82 Q16 89 16 112 L29 116 L43 94Z" fill="#34596d" stroke="#d5bd86" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M18 109 L18 119 L29 121 L32 112Z" fill="#f0c7a0" stroke="#c2936c" stroke-width="1.5"/></g>
 <g class="exp23-body"><path d="M31 80 Q53 71 79 80 L85 118 Q57 133 27 116Z" fill="url(#exp23-cloth)" stroke="#f4d89e" stroke-width="2.7"/><path d="M36 85 Q57 99 76 84" stroke="#d7dfd2" stroke-width="3" fill="none"/><path d="M29 108 L81 110 L82 117 L29 115Z" fill="#684f3c" stroke="#f2cf81" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="55" cy="113" r="5.4" fill="url(#exp23-metal)"/><path d="M43 89 L56 102 L70 87" stroke="url(#exp23-metal)" stroke-width="4" fill="none"/><path d="M50 103 L57 107 L63 101 L58 115 L54 119Z" fill="#f1d98b"/></g>
 <g class="exp23-head"><path d="M42 67 L38 87 Q57 96 73 83 L69 65Z" fill="#e6bb95"/><path d="M30 43 Q27 17 55 17 Q83 19 82 48 L77 68 Q67 83 50 79 Q31 71 30 43Z" fill="#f2c7a6" stroke="#a47c62" stroke-width="1.5"/><path d="M30 43 Q20 7 55 9 Q89 7 85 52 Q80 46 76 30 Q52 37 39 33 L34 58Z" fill="#30364b" stroke="#b69f8a" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M32 35 Q43 9 76 20 L74 28 Q51 25 37 46Z" fill="#586476"/><path d="M55 49 Q60 47 63 50" stroke="#5d5149" stroke-width="2" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M71 46 L75 48" stroke="#5d5149" stroke-width="2.1" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M61 65 Q68 69 73 64" stroke="#945f4b" stroke-width="1.6" fill="none"/><ellipse cx="38" cy="60" rx="5" ry="3" fill="#f4a78c" opacity=".32"/></g>
 <g class="exp23-arm exp23-arm-front"><path d="M75 82 Q94 86 92 111 L83 117 L69 93Z" fill="#2e475a" stroke="#f1cf8d" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M83 109 L84 121 L95 118 L95 108Z" fill="#eac09f" stroke="#ab8366" stroke-width="1.5"/>
 <g class="exp23-hand-lamp"><circle cx="94" cy="126" r="26" fill="url(#exp23-lantern)"/><path d="M89 116 L100 116 L101 133 L88 133Z" fill="#473e38" stroke="#ffe0a0" stroke-width="2.5"/><path d="M91 114 Q93 107 98 113" stroke="#e8d39d" stroke-width="2.3" fill="none"/><rect x="91" y="120" width="8" height="10" rx="1" fill="#ffe9a5"/><circle cx="94" cy="125" r="3" fill="#fffce2"/></g></g>
 </svg></div>`;}
 function install(viewport){
  const sc=viewport.querySelector('.stage-scene');if(!sc)return;
  const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.className='exp22-world';
  layer.setAttribute('aria-label','時計塔の探索エリア');
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="exp23-background" aria-hidden="true"><span class="exp23-mist"></span><span class="exp23-light exp23-light-a"></span><span class="exp23-light exp23-light-b"></span><span class="exp23-columns"></span><span class="exp23-floor"></span><span class="exp23-embers"></span></div><div class="exp22-guide" id="exp22Guide" role="status">時計塔の奥にある振り子装置へ行こう</div>
  <div class="exp22-character" role="img" aria-label="時計塔を探検する主人公">${makeAvatar()}</div>
  <button type="button" class="exp22-apparatus" aria-label="振り子の制御装置へ向かう"><span class="exp22-sigil" aria-hidden="true">⚙</span><span>振り子制御装置</span></button>
  <div class="exp22-foot-controls" role="group" aria-label="キャラクターの移動操作">
   <button type="button" data-expdir="up" aria-label="奥へ歩く">▲</button>
   <button type="button" data-expdir="left" aria-label="左へ歩く">◀</button>
   <span class="exp22-core" aria-hidden="true">✦</span>
   <button type="button" data-expdir="right" aria-label="右へ歩く">▶</button>
   <button type="button" data-expdir="down" aria-label="手前へ歩く">▼</button>
  </div>
  <button type="button" class="exp22-interact" id="exp22Interact" data-near="false">→ 装置に近づく</button>
  <div class="exp22-note">移動：方向キー／WASD／十字キー</div>
  <div class="exp23-world-chip" aria-hidden="true"><span>◈</span><div>THE CLOCKTOWER<small>時を取り戻す冒険</small></div></div>`;
  sc.append(layer);viewport.classList.add('exp22-enabled','exp23-exploring');
  viewport.classList.remove('exp23-experiment');
  function setStation(open){
   stationOpen=!!open;
   viewport.classList.toggle('exp23-exploring',!stationOpen);
   viewport.classList.toggle('exp23-experiment',stationOpen);
   const command=viewport.querySelector('[data-hud-action="controls"]');
   if(stationOpen){
    pendingInteract=false;target=null;held.clear();
    if(command&&!viewport.classList.contains('commands-open'))command.click();
    setGuide('観察しながら長さを変えよう。終わったら探索へ戻れるよ。');
   }else{
    if(viewport.classList.contains('commands-open'))viewport.querySelector('[data-hud-action="close"]')?.click();
    setGuide('時計塔を歩いて、光る装置を調べよう。');
   }
   updateVisual(false);
  }
  const doInteract=()=>{if(!isActive())return;
   if(stationOpen){setStation(false);return;}
   if(!isNear()){exp28Destination=null;target={x:machine.x-.09,z:machine.z-.03};pendingInteract=true;setGuide('制御装置まで自動で歩いているよ…');return;}
   setStation(true);
  };
  // The existing HUD remains usable without requiring a second interaction route.
  layer.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('.exp23-return'))setStation(false)});
  layer.querySelector('.exp22-interact').addEventListener('click',doInteract);
  layer.querySelector('.exp22-apparatus').addEventListener('click',doInteract);
  for(const btn of layer.querySelectorAll('[data-expdir]')){
   const dir=btn.dataset.expdir;
   const down=e=>{if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;e.preventDefault();target=null;exp28Destination=null;held.add(dir);btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');try{btn.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}};
   const up=()=>{held.delete(dir);btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false')};
   btn.addEventListener('pointerdown',down);['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'].forEach(ev=>btn.addEventListener(ev,up));
   btn.addEventListener('blur',up);
  }
  current={viewport,layer,avatar:layer.querySelector('.exp22-character'),beacon:layer.querySelector('.exp22-apparatus'),action:layer.querySelector('#exp22Interact'),guide:layer.querySelector('#exp22Guide')};
  player.x=.49;player.z=.12;player.face=1;target=null;exp28Destination=null;held.clear();nearBefore=null;activeBefore=false;
  stationOpen=viewport.classList.contains('commands-open');pendingInteract=false;cameraX=0;cameraY=0;lastDirection='down';
  viewport.classList.toggle('exp23-exploring',!stationOpen);
  viewport.classList.toggle('exp23-experiment',stationOpen);
  current.openStation=()=>setStation(true);
  updateVisual(false);
 }
 function setGuide(message){if(current&&current.guide.textContent!==message)current.guide.textContent=message;}
 function isNear(){return Math.hypot((player.x-machine.x)*1.05,(player.z-machine.z)*1.35)<.18}
 function updateVisual(moving){if(!current)return;
  const near=isNear(),done=!!(state.progress&&state.progress.s01);
  const scale=.74+player.z*.44;
  const av=current.avatar;
  // The camera follows the player, rather than pinning the sprite to a static background.
  av.style.left=(50+(player.x-.5)*54)+'%';av.style.bottom=(23+player.z*19)+'%';
  current.beacon.style.left=(76+(.5-player.x)*34)+'%';
  current.beacon.style.bottom=(30+(.5-player.z)*3)+'%';
  av.style.setProperty('--exp22-scale',String(scale));av.style.setProperty('--exp22-facing',String(player.face));
  av.dataset.direction=lastDirection;
  av.classList.toggle('walking',moving&&!stationOpen);av.classList.toggle('near',near);
  current.viewport.style.setProperty('--exp-cam-x',cameraX.toFixed(1)+'px');
  current.viewport.style.setProperty('--exp23-camera',cameraX.toFixed(1)+'px');
  current.viewport.style.setProperty('--exp23-depth',cameraY.toFixed(1)+'px');
  current.viewport.dataset.restored=done?'yes':'no';
  current.beacon.classList.toggle('near',near);current.beacon.classList.toggle('done',done);
  const action=current.action;action.dataset.near=String(near);
  const label=stationOpen?'← 探索にもどる':done?'✦ 装置を再調整':near?'⚙ 振り子を調べる':'→ 装置に向かう';
  if(action.textContent!==label)action.textContent=label;
  if(!stationOpen&&(near!==nearBefore||done!==activeBefore)){
   setGuide(done?'装置は作動中！ もう一度実験することもできる。':near?'⚙ 装置の前に到着。調べてみよう。':'十字キーで振り子制御装置へ向かおう。');
   nearBefore=near;activeBefore=done;
  }
 }
 function activeForKeyboard(e){
  if(!isActive()||!current||current.viewport.classList.contains('commands-open'))return false;
  const el=e.target;if(el?.closest?.('input,textarea,select,[contenteditable="true"],button,a'))return false;
  return true;
 }
 const keys={ArrowLeft:'left',ArrowRight:'right',ArrowUp:'up',ArrowDown:'down',a:'left',d:'right',w:'up',s:'down'};
 document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(!activeForKeyboard(e))return;
  const dir=keys[e.key];if(dir){e.preventDefault();target=null;exp28Destination=null;held.add(dir)}
  else if((e.key==='e'||e.key==='E')&&current){e.preventDefault();current.action.click();}
 });
 document.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{const dir=keys[e.key];if(dir)held.delete(dir)});
 window.addEventListener('blur',()=>held.clear());
 function frame(ms){
  const dt=clamp((ms-last)/1000,0,.04);last=ms;
  if(isActive()){
   const vp=app.querySelector('.stage-viewport');
   if(vp&&vp!==current?.viewport)install(vp);
   if(current&&vp===current.viewport){
    let dx=0,dz=0;
    if(!vp.classList.contains('commands-open')){
     dx=(held.has('right')?1:0)-(held.has('left')?1:0);
     dz=(held.has('up')?1:0)-(held.has('down')?1:0);
     if(!dx&&!dz&&target){const tx=target.x-player.x,tz=target.z-player.z;
       if(Math.hypot(tx,tz)<.02)target=null;else{dx=tx;dz=tz;}
     }
    }
    const norm=Math.hypot(dx,dz),moving=norm>.001;
    if(moving){const speed=.33*dt;const vx=dx/norm*speed,vz=dz/norm*speed;
      const oldx=player.x,oldz=player.z;
      player.x=clamp(player.x+vx,.07,.93);player.z=clamp(player.z+vz,.04,.82);
      if(target&&Math.hypot(player.x-target.x,player.z-target.z)<.018)target=null;
      if(Math.abs(player.x-oldx)>.0001)player.face=player.x>oldx?1:-1;
      if(Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dz))lastDirection=dx>0?'right':'left';
      else lastDirection=dz>0?'up':'down';
    }
    cameraX+=((.5-player.x)*132-cameraX)*Math.min(1,dt*5);
    cameraY+=((player.z-.12)*28-cameraY)*Math.min(1,dt*4);
    if(pendingInteract&&isNear()&&!stationOpen){pendingInteract=false;current.openStation?.();}
    if(exp28Destination&&Math.hypot(player.x-exp28Destination.x,player.z-exp28Destination.z)<.042){
     const id=exp28Destination.id;exp28Destination=null;target=null;
     app.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('furiko:explore-arrived',{detail:{id}}));
    }
    updateVisual(moving);
    if(ms-exp28LastBroadcast>95){exp28LastBroadcast=ms;
     app.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('furiko:explore-position',{detail:{x:player.x,z:player.z,moving,experiment:stationOpen}}));
    }
   }
  }else if(current){current=null;held.clear();target=null;nearBefore=null;}
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
 }
 app.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const control=e.target.closest?.('[data-hud-action]');if(!control||!current)return;
  if(control.dataset.hudAction==='observe'||control.dataset.hudAction==='close'){
   if(stationOpen){stationOpen=false;current.viewport.classList.add('exp23-exploring');current.viewport.classList.remove('exp23-experiment');}
  }else if(control.dataset.hudAction==='controls'&&!stationOpen){
   stationOpen=true;pendingInteract=false;current.viewport.classList.add('exp23-experiment');current.viewport.classList.remove('exp23-exploring');
  }
 });
 app.addEventListener('furiko:explore-go',e=>{
  if(!isActive()||!current||stationOpen)return;
  const d=e.detail||{};
  if(typeof d.x!=='number'||typeof d.z!=='number'||!d.id)return;
  pendingInteract=false;held.clear();
  exp28Destination={id:d.id,x:clamp(d.x,.07,.93),z:clamp(d.z,.04,.82)};
  target={x:exp28Destination.x,z:exp28Destination.z};
 });
 requestAnimationFrame(frame);
})();
</script>
